**Text**

Future Arriving
Space
Robotaxis
Robotics
Telco
Apptronik

**Layout**

Four equal portrait image columns with green category labels centred above each, on a black field; the title is centred near the top, and the NVIDIA logo sits at bottom right. Columns are evenly spaced with margins of about 5% left and right.

**Visuals**

Four portrait photographs in rounded boxes, left to right. (1) Space: Earth from orbit with a small satellite at upper left, a bright gold-yellow atmospheric limb arc along the upper right, city lights glowing across the dark night side at lower left, black space above; cool blue and white tones. (2) Robotaxis: a light silver-white electric sedan viewed from front three-quarter, with a woman passenger in pink top visible through the windshield, on a city street with trees, apartment buildings, two cyclists and parked cars behind; blue radar-like arcs drawn on the road, green lane tint at left and right, bright white road markings. (3) Robotics: a white and grey humanoid robot with a rounded head and two camera eyes, standing behind a white cylindrical home robot on a wooden table next to a clear drinking glass; kitchen background with grey cabinetry and a range hood; small 'Apptronik' caption at lower right. (4) Telco: a tall steel lattice telecom tower with multiple antenna panels, against blue sky with white clouds, rolling green forested hills and a small red-roofed town below; bright daylight. All photographs are boxed with thin rounded edges, not cropped to bleed.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000.

**Layout**

Full-bleed black slide with a centred title and three centred subtitle lines across the top. Below, two equal rounded-corner panels sit side by side: a left diagram panel with three circular illustrations in a triangle, and a right bar chart panel. The NVIDIA logo sits bottom right; no slide number is visible.

**Visuals**

Left panel: a triangular cycle diagram. Top-left circle (Training, DGX) holds a white line sketch of server racks with gold-tinted highlights. Top-right circle (Simulation, Omniverse / Cosmos) holds a white line sketch of humanoid robots on steps. Bottom-centre circle holds a photograph of Earth from space with a gold and silver car sketch and a robot figure with raised hand overlaid. Green curved double-headed arrows connect Training to Simulation and each circle to the Earth. Right panel: stacked column chart titled NVIDIA Physical AI Revenue ($ Billions). Y axis 0.0 to 20.0 in steps of 4.0, no unit beyond $ Billions in the title. X axis categories FY22, FY23, FY24, FY25, FY26, FY27 (1H). Printed totals: FY22 2.9, FY23 3.3, FY24 5.2, FY25 9.3, FY26 8.2, FY27 (1H) 10.8. Segments are not labelled; about values for FY27 (1H), bottom to top: Auto about 6.4, Healthcare about 0.8, Manufacturing about 1.4, Smart Spaces about 0.8, Telco about 1.1, Robotics about 0.3. Series colours: Auto #76B900, Healthcare #1DBBA4, Manufacturing #0074DF, Smart Spaces #952FC6, Telco #EF9100, Robotics #757575. Legend sits in one row above the plot. FY27 column is highlighted with a lime #B8FF3C outline and a dark-to-grey gradient strip above it, and a rounded callout box with a thin lime leader line points to it.

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000. Pure black #000000 background. Title white #FFFFFF in NVIDIASans Medium, sans-serif, about 4.9 percent of slide height. Subtitle lines grey #A1A1A1 in NVIDIASans Regular, about 2.0 percent of height. Panel labels white bold NVIDIASans Bold about 1.7 percent. Chart labels white NVIDIASans Regular about 1.5 to 1.7 percent. Accent green #76B900 and lime #B8FF3C. Panels have thin grey #3A3A3A strokes, small corner radius, no shadows.

### Slide 28

**Review notes**

- Review: The deck reads better as a pre-read than as a projected presentation, and the closing slide 28 is a plain text block that ends weakly.
- What would change the score: I would rebuild slide 28 as a visual summary tied to the three growth pillars, and cut the intro paragraphs above the charts on slides 11 and 15 to one line each.

**Text**

NVIDIA: A Platform for Compounding Growth and Value
NVIDIA is a platform that delivers compounding growth and value to investors.
CUDA programmability underpins the productivity, fungibility, and durability of NVIDIA compute. Our platform addresses every model, workload, and deployment. As a result, NVIDIA is increasing share within an expanding AI market.
NVIDIA is also capturing more value per GW driven by advancements in the GPU and a broadening product portfolio – a reflection of NVIDIA's strategy in becoming a full-stack AI factory platform.
And as the platform broadens from chip to a full-stack AI factory, NVIDIA captures a growing % of customers' IT CapEx.
Together, these three growth pillars will drive an increase in NVIDIA's cash generation and capital return to shareholders.

**Layout**

Single full-bleed black slide with one text column. The title is centred horizontally near the top, about 10.5% from the top edge. Body paragraphs are left-aligned at about 5.2% from the left edge, starting at about 26.6% from the top and stacked down to about 71% with uneven vertical gaps between paragraphs. The lower third is empty. A small NVIDIA logo sits in the bottom-right corner. There is no footer text and no slide number.

**Visuals**

none

**Design**

Background: Solid black #000000 fill covering the whole slide. Solid black background. Title in NVIDIASans Medium, white #FFFFFF, roughly 4.9% of slide height. Body text in NVIDIASans Regular, grey #A1A1A1, roughly 2.7% of slide height. Emphasised phrases are set in NVIDIASans Bold in NVIDIA green #76B900 inline within the grey body lines. No shapes, rules, gradients, shadows or corner radii. Only the logo uses green.

### Slide 29

**Text**

NVIDIA

**Layout**

Full-bleed photographic background with the NVIDIA logo lockup placed in the lower-left area; no title, body text or slide number. The 3D green logo mark sits on the right third and bleeds off the right and top edges.

**Visuals**

Photograph-style 3D render: a large glossy green NVIDIA eye-shaped logo mark, extruded and seen at an oblique angle, floating over a dark field of faceted, triangular black tiles that recede toward the upper left. Lighting is directional from the upper right, producing bright specular highlights on the green edges and a grey-blue sheen on the upper-right tiles. Composition is landscape, the logo cropped by the right and top edges. No overlay or tint beyond natural darkness. Small NVIDIA logo lockup at lower left: a green eye mark followed by the white wordmark NVIDIA.

**Design**

Background: Near-black #0A0C0E faceted 3D triangular tile surface, photographed/rendered behind all content. Dark, near-black background with faceted triangular 3D texture. Accent colour bright NVIDIA green #76B900 for the logo mark. Wordmark in white #FFFFFF, bold geometric sans-serif capitals. No titles or body text. No rounded corners, borders or shadows beyond the render's own soft shading.
